*Licheng Luo,Kaier Liang,Cristian-Ioan Vasile,Mingyu Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Differentiable SpaTiaL，首个端到端可微分的符号时空逻辑工具箱，通过构建平滑的几何基元实现从高级语义规范到底层几何配置的完全可微映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中执行复杂操作需要满足耦合的几何和时间约束。现有可微分时序逻辑关注机器人内部状态而忽略交互对象-环境关系，而捕获这种交互的空间逻辑方法依赖离散几何引擎，破坏了计算图并阻碍精确梯度传播。

Method: 提出完全张量化的工具箱，直接在多边形集合上构建平滑、自动梯度兼容的几何基元。通过解析推导关键空间谓词（包括符号距离、相交、包含和方向关系）的可微分松弛，实现端到端可微分映射。

Result: 实验验证了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。该完全可微分公式解锁了两个核心能力：(1) 在严格时空约束下的大规模并行轨迹优化；(2) 通过反向传播直接从演示中学习空间逻辑参数。

Conclusion: Differentiable SpaTiaL是首个端到端可微分的符号时空逻辑工具箱，通过构建平滑几何基元实现从高级语义规范到底层几何配置的完全可微映射，为复杂操作任务提供了新的优化和学习能力。

Abstract: Executing complex manipulation in cluttered environments requires satisfying coupled geometric and temporal constraints. Although Spatio-Temporal Logic (SpaTiaL) offers a principled specification framework, its use in gradient-based optimization is limited by non-differentiable geometric operations. Existing differentiable temporal logics focus on the robot's internal state and neglect interactive object-environment relations, while spatial logic approaches that capture such interactions rely on discrete geometry engines that break the computational graph and preclude exact gradient propagation. To overcome this limitation, we propose Differentiable SpaTiaL, a fully tensorized toolbox that constructs smooth, autograd-compatible geometric primitives directly over polygonal sets. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end differentiable symbolic spatio-temporal logic toolbox. By analytically deriving differentiable relaxations of key spatial predicates--including signed distance, intersection, containment, and directional relations--we enable an end-to-end differentiable mapping from high-level semantic specifications to low-level geometric configurations, without invoking external discrete solvers. This fully differentiable formulation unlocks two core capabilities: (i) massively parallel trajectory optimization under rigorous spatio-temporal constraints, and (ii) direct learning of spatial logic parameters from demonstrations via backpropagation. Experimental results validate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed framework.Code Available: https://github.com/plen1lune/DiffSpaTiaL

</details>


### [114] [ALIVE-LIO: Degeneracy-Aware Learning of Inertial Velocity for Enhancing ESKF-Based LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02706)
*Seongjun Kim,Daehan Lee,Junwoo Hong,Sanghyun Park,Hyunyoung Jo,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALIVE-LIO：一种退化感知的LiDAR-IMU里程计框架，通过神经网络预测速度并仅在检测到退化时选择性融合到ESKF中，以增强退化环境下的状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR-IMU里程计（LIO）在退化环境（如长走廊、单墙场景）中性能会显著下降，因为LiDAR在这些方向上的可观测性不足。

Method: 将深度神经网络集成到经典误差状态卡尔曼滤波（ESKF）中，神经网络预测机体坐标系速度，仅在检测到退化时选择性融合到ESKF中，为退化方向提供有效状态更新。

Result: 在公开数据集和自采集数据上的实验表明，ALIVE-LIO显著减少了退化环境中的姿态漂移，在32个序列中的22个取得了最具竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: ALIVE-LIO成功解决了LIO在退化环境中的性能问题，通过选择性融合神经网络预测，既保持了ESKF的概率结构和一致性，又受益于基于学习的运动估计。

Abstract: Odometry estimation using light detection and ranging (LiDAR) and an inertial measurement unit (IMU), known as LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO), often suffers from performance degradation in degenerate environments, such as long corridors or single-wall scenarios with narrow field-of-view LiDAR. To address this limitation, we propose ALIVE-LIO, a degeneracy-aware LiDAR-inertial odometry framework that explicitly enhances state estimation in degenerate directions. The key contribution of ALIVE-LIO is the strategic integration of a deep neural network into a classical error-state Kalman filter (ESKF) to compensate for the loss of LiDAR observability. Specifically, ALIVE-LIO employs a neural network to predict the body-frame velocity and selectively fuses this prediction into the ESKF only when degeneracy is detected, providing effective state updates along degenerate directions. This design enables ALIVE-LIO to utilize the probabilistic structure and consistency of the ESKF while benefiting from learning-based motion estimation. The proposed method was evaluated on publicly available datasets exhibiting degeneracy, as well as on our own collected data. Experimental results demonstrate that ALIVE-LIO substantially reduces pose drift in degenerate environments, yielding the most competitive results in 22 out of 32 sequences. The implementation of ALIVE-LIO will be publicly available.

</details>


### [115] [A Rapid Instrument Exchange System for Humanoid Robots in Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02707)
*Bingcong Zhang,Yihang Lyv,Lianbo Ma,Yushi He,Pengfei Wei,Xingchi Liu,Jinhua Li,Jianchang Zhao,Lizhi Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出沉浸式遥操作快速器械交换系统，用于人形机器人手术，通过单轴柔性对接和环境约束释放机制降低操作复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人具有巨大微创手术潜力，但其固有的双臂配置需要高效的器械交换能力来执行复杂手术，模拟外科医生手动切换器械的自然工作流程

Method: 基于单轴柔性对接和环境约束释放的低延迟机制，结合头戴式显示器实时第一人称视角感知的沉浸式遥操作系统

Result: 专家与新手的对比评估显示系统具有高操作鲁棒性和快速收敛的学习曲线，新手经过简短训练后器械装卸性能显著提升

Conclusion: 虽然长距离空间对齐在时间成本和协作稳定性方面仍存在挑战，但研究成功验证了人形机器人在受限临床环境中执行稳定器械交换的技术可行性

Abstract: Humanoid robot technologies have demonstrated immense potential for minimally invasive surgery (MIS). Unlike dedicated multi-arm surgical platforms, the inherent dual-arm configuration of humanoid robots necessitates an efficient instrument exchange capability to perform complex procedures, mimicking the natural workflow where surgeons manually switch instruments. To address this, this paper proposes an immersive teleoperated rapid instrument exchange system. The system utilizes a low-latency mechanism based on single-axis compliant docking and environmental constraint release. Integrated with real-time first-person view (FPV) perception via a head-mounted display (HMD), this framework significantly reduces operational complexity and cognitive load during the docking process. Comparative evaluations between experts and novices demonstrate high operational robustness and a rapidly converging learning curve; novice performance in instrument attachment and detachment improved substantially after brief training. While long-distance spatial alignment still presents challenges in time cost and collaborative stability, this study successfully validates the technical feasibility of humanoid robots executing stable instrument exchanges within constrained clinical environments.

</details>


### [116] [V2X-QA: A Comprehensive Reasoning Dataset and Benchmark for Multimodal Large Language Models in Autonomous Driving Across Ego, Infrastructure, and Cooperative Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02710)
*Junwei You,Pei Li,Zhuoyu Jiang,Weizhe Tang,Zilin Huang,Rui Gan,Jiaxi Liu,Yan Zhao,Sikai Chen,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了V2X-QA数据集和基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在车路协同自动驾驶中的多视角推理能力，包括车辆侧、路侧和协同视角，并提出了V2X-MoE基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶基准主要关注单车视角，无法系统评估模型在路侧和车路协同条件下的性能，需要建立能够评估多视角推理能力的基准。

Method: 构建V2X-QA真实世界数据集，采用视图解耦评估协议，在统一的多选题框架下支持车辆侧、路侧和协同驾驶条件的对比评估。数据集包含12个任务分类，涵盖感知、预测、推理和规划。同时提出V2X-MoE基准模型，采用显式视图路由和视图特定的LoRA专家。

Result: 对10个代表性SOTA模型的评估显示：视角可访问性显著影响性能；路侧推理支持有意义的宏观交通理解；协同推理仍具挑战性，需要跨视图对齐和证据整合。V2X-MoE模型表现出色，表明显式视图专业化是多视角推理的有前景方向。

Conclusion: V2X-QA为研究车路协同自动驾驶中的多视角推理、可靠性和协同物理智能提供了基础，V2X-MoE模型验证了显式视图专业化方法的有效性，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for autonomous driving, yet existing benchmarks remain largely ego-centric and therefore cannot systematically assess model performance in infrastructure-centric and cooperative driving conditions. In this work, we introduce V2X-QA, a real-world dataset and benchmark for evaluating MLLMs across vehicle-side, infrastructure-side, and cooperative viewpoints. V2X-QA is built around a view-decoupled evaluation protocol that enables controlled comparison under vehicle-only, infrastructure-only, and cooperative driving conditions within a unified multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) framework. The benchmark is organized into a twelve-task taxonomy spanning perception, prediction, and reasoning and planning, and is constructed through expert-verified MCQA annotation to enable fine-grained diagnosis of viewpoint-dependent capabilities. Benchmark results across ten representative state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source models show that viewpoint accessibility substantially affects performance, and infrastructure-side reasoning supports meaningful macroscopic traffic understanding. Results also indicate that cooperative reasoning remains challenging since it requires cross-view alignment and evidence integration rather than simply additional visual input. To address these challenges, we introduce V2X-MoE, a benchmark-aligned baseline with explicit view routing and viewpoint-specific LoRA experts. The strong performance of V2X-MoE further suggests that explicit viewpoint specialization is a promising direction for multi-view reasoning in autonomous driving. Overall, V2X-QA provides a foundation for studying multi-perspective reasoning, reliability, and cooperative physical intelligence in connected autonomous driving. The dataset and V2X-MoE resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/junwei0001/V2X-QA.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Locomotion on Complex Terrain for Quadrupedal Robots with Foot Position Maps and Stability Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02744)
*Matthew Hwang,Yubin Liu,Ryo Hakoda,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合足部位置映射和动态稳定性奖励的注意力框架，用于四足机器人在复杂地形上的精确稳定运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖关节角度隐式推断足部位置，缺乏优化方法的显式精度和稳定性保证，需要改进复杂地形下的四足运动

Method: 引入集成到高度图中的足部位置映射，结合动态运动稳定性奖励，在注意力框架中实现复杂地形上的精确运动控制

Result: 在训练期间见过的地形和域外地形上验证，方法实现了精确稳定的运动，提高了训练域内和域外地形的运动成功率

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过显式足部位置映射和稳定性奖励，在复杂地形上实现了优于现有强化学习方法的精确稳定四足运动

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion over complex terrain has been a long-standing research topic in robotics. While recent reinforcement learning-based locomotion methods improve generalizability and foot-placement precision, they rely on implicit inference of foot positions from joint angles, lacking the explicit precision and stability guarantees of optimization-based approaches. To address this, we introduce a foot position map integrated into the heightmap, and a dynamic locomotion-stability reward within an attention-based framework to achieve locomotion on complex terrain. We validate our method extensively on terrains seen during training as well as out-of-domain (OOD) terrains. Our results demonstrate that the proposed method enables precise and stable movement, resulting in improved locomotion success rates on both in-domain and OOD terrains.

</details>


### [118] [Geometrically-Constrained Radar-Inertial Odometry via Continuous Point-Pose Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02745)
*Wooseong Yang,Dongjae Lee,Minwoo Jung,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出几何约束的雷达-惯性里程计与建图方法，通过联合处理点和位姿不确定性，在稀疏雷达数据和噪声环境下实现鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达里程计在恶劣环境中对鲁棒定位至关重要，但可靠的雷达回波稀疏且噪声特性独特，限制了其性能。现有方法难以有效处理雷达测量的不确定性。

Method: 采用连续轨迹模型估计任意时间戳的位姿不确定性，通过控制点传播不确定性。将位姿不确定性与异方差测量不确定性在点投影中连续集成，实现观测置信度的动态评估和非信息雷达点的自适应降权。利用量化的不确定性构建高保真地图。

Result: 在多样化真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和效率方面均优于现有基线，取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出的几何约束雷达-惯性里程计与不确定性感知建图框架有效提升了雷达定位性能，特别是在雷达测量不精确的情况下，几何约束与不确定性处理相结合显示出显著优势。

Abstract: Radar odometry is crucial for robust localization in challenging environments; however, the sparsity of reliable returns and distinctive noise characteristics impede its performance. This paper introduces geometrically-constrained radar-inertial odometry and mapping that jointly consolidates point and pose uncertainty. We employ the continuous trajectory model to estimate the pose uncertainty at any arbitrary timestamp by propagating uncertainties of the control points. These pose uncertainties are continuously integrated with heteroscedastic measurement uncertainty during point projection, thereby enabling dynamic evaluation of observation confidence and adaptive down-weighting of uninformative radar points. By leveraging quantified uncertainties in radar mapping, we construct a high-fidelity map that improves odometry accuracy under imprecise radar measurements. Moreover, we reveal the effectiveness of explicit geometrical constraints in radar-inertial odometry when incorporated with the proposed uncertainty-aware mapping framework. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of our method, yielding substantial performance improvements in both accuracy and efficiency compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [119] [OMNI-PoseX: A Fast Vision Model for 6D Object Pose Estimation in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02759)
*Michael Zhang,Wei Ying,Fangwen Chen,Shifeng Bai,Hanwen Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMNI-PoseX是一个视觉基础模型，通过结合开放词汇感知和SO(3)感知反射流匹配姿态预测器，实现了开放世界中高效稳定的6D物体姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6D物体姿态估计方法通常依赖闭集假设或几何不可知的回归方案，限制了它们在开放世界环境中的泛化能力、稳定性和实时应用性，特别是在机器人系统中。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的网络架构，将开放词汇感知与SO(3)感知反射流匹配姿态预测器统一起来。该架构将物体级理解与几何一致的旋转推断解耦，采用轻量级多模态融合策略，将旋转敏感的几何特征与紧凑语义嵌入相结合。

Result: 在基准姿态估计、消融研究、零样本泛化和系统级机器人抓取集成等全面评估中表现出色。OMNI-PoseX实现了最先进的姿态精度和实时效率，同时提供几何一致的预测，能够可靠抓取各种未见过的物体。

Conclusion: OMNI-PoseX作为一个视觉基础模型，通过统一开放词汇感知和几何感知的姿态预测，为开放世界环境中的6D物体姿态估计提供了高效、稳定且泛化能力强的解决方案，特别适用于机器人系统。

Abstract: Accurate 6D object pose estimation is a fundamental capability for embodied agents, yet remains highly challenging in open-world environments. Many existing methods often rely on closed-set assumptions or geometry-agnostic regression schemes, limiting their generalization, stability, and real-time applicability in robotic systems. We present OMNI-PoseX, a vision foundation model that introduces a novel network architecture unifying open-vocabulary perception with an SO(3)-aware reflected flow matching pose predictor. The architecture decouples object-level understanding from geometry-consistent rotation inference, and employs a lightweight multi-modal fusion strategy that conditions rotation-sensitive geometric features on compact semantic embeddings, enabling efficient and stable 6D pose estimation. To enhance robustness and generalization, the model is trained on large-scale 6D pose datasets, leveraging broad object diversity, viewpoint variation, and scene complexity to build a scalable open-world pose backbone. Comprehensive evaluations across benchmark pose estimation, ablation studies, zero-shot generalization, and system-level robotic grasping integration demonstrate the effectiveness of OMNI-PoseX. The OMNI-PoseX achieves SOTA pose accuracy and real-time efficiency, while delivering geometrically consistent predictions that enable reliable grasping of diverse, previously unseen objects.

</details>


### [120] [Vision-Based End-to-End Learning for UAV Traversal of Irregular Gaps via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02779)
*Linzuo Zhang,Yu Hu,Feng Yu,Yang Deng,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个完全基于视觉的端到端框架，让无人机能够直接根据深度图像生成控制指令，穿越复杂且未见过的环境中的不规则缝隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖显式的缝隙提取和测量，而现有的端到端方法通常假设缝隙形状规则，导致泛化能力差和实用性有限。无人机在检查、搜救和灾难响应等应用中需要穿越狭窄不规则缝隙的能力。

Method: 采用完全基于视觉的端到端框架，将深度图像直接映射到控制指令。在SE(3)空间中运行，结合可微分模拟、Stop-Gradient算子和双峰初始化分布来实现稳定穿越。还包含两个辅助预测模块：穿越成功分类器和可穿越性预测器。

Result: 通过大量模拟和真实世界实验验证了方法的有效性、泛化能力和实际鲁棒性，能够稳定穿越连续的不规则缝隙。

Conclusion: 该框架为无人机在复杂未知环境中穿越不规则缝隙提供了一种实用且泛化能力强的解决方案，结合了端到端学习的优势与辅助安全模块的可靠性。

Abstract: -Navigation through narrow and irregular gaps is an essential skill in autonomous drones for applications such as inspection, search-and-rescue, and disaster response. However, traditional planning and control methods rely on explicit gap extraction and measurement, while recent end-to-end approaches often assume regularly shaped gaps, leading to poor generalization and limited practicality. In this work, we present a fully vision-based, end-to-end framework that maps depth images directly to control commands, enabling drones to traverse complex gaps within unseen environments. Operating in the Special Euclidean group SE(3), where position and orientation are tightly coupled, the framework leverages differentiable simulation, a Stop-Gradient operator, and a Bimodal Initialization Distribution to achieve stable traversal through consecutive gaps. Two auxiliary prediction modules-a gap-crossing success classifier and a traversability predictor-further enhance continuous navigation and safety. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the approach's effectiveness, generalization capability, and practical robustness.

</details>


### [121] [QuadAgent: A Responsive Agent System for Vision-Language Guided Quadrotor Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02786)
*Ao Zhuang,Feng Yu,Tianbao Zhang,Linzuo Zhang,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuadAgent是一个无需训练的无人机智能体系统，采用异步多智能体架构，将高层推理与底层控制解耦，通过视觉语言输入指导敏捷飞行，在复杂室内环境中可达5m/s速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端或串行智能体方法在无人机敏捷飞行任务中存在局限性，需要一种能够处理复杂视觉语言指令、进行前瞻性推理并确保安全飞行的系统。

Method: 采用异步多智能体架构：前景工作流智能体处理主动任务和用户指令，背景智能体进行前瞻推理。系统通过Impression Graph（基于稀疏关键帧构建的轻量拓扑地图）维护场景记忆，并使用基于视觉的避障网络确保飞行安全。

Result: 仿真结果显示QuadAgent在效率和响应性上优于基线方法。真实世界实验表明系统能够解释复杂指令、推理周围环境，并在杂乱室内空间中以高达5m/s的速度导航。

Conclusion: QuadAgent通过创新的异步多智能体架构和场景记忆系统，实现了无需训练的无人机敏捷飞行控制，能够有效处理视觉语言指令并在复杂环境中安全高速导航。

Abstract: We present QuadAgent, a training-free agent system for agile quadrotor flight guided by vision-language inputs. Unlike prior end-to-end or serial agent approaches, QuadAgent decouples high-level reasoning from low-level control using an asynchronous multi-agent architecture: Foreground Workflow Agents handle active tasks and user commands, while Background Agents perform look-ahead reasoning. The system maintains scene memory via the Impression Graph, a lightweight topological map built from sparse keyframes, and ensures safe flight with a vision-based obstacle avoidance network. Simulation results show that QuadAgent outperforms baseline methods in efficiency and responsiveness. Real-world experiments demonstrate that it can interpret complex instructions, reason about its surroundings, and navigate cluttered indoor spaces at speeds up to 5 m/s.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Structured Robot Policies from Vision-Language Models via Synthetic Neuro-Symbolic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02812)
*Alessandro Adami,Tommaso Tubaldo,Marco Todescato,Ruggero Carli,Pietro Falco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号方法，让视觉语言模型从多模态观察生成可执行的Behavior Tree策略，通过合成数据集训练，实现从仿真到真实世界的策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型的机器人控制大多采用端到端策略，缺乏可解释性和模块化，难以应用于安全关键场景。而传统机器人系统依赖结构化策略表示，具有可解释性、模块化和反应式执行的优势。

Method: 提出神经符号方法：使用VLM从视觉观察、自然语言指令和结构化系统规范合成可执行的Behavior Tree策略。引入自动化流水线生成合成多模态数据集，包含域随机化场景和由基础模型生成的指令-策略对，无需人工标注。

Result: 在两个机器人操作器上进行真实世界实验，表明完全从合成监督学习的结构化策略能够成功迁移到物理系统。基础模型能够生成可解释的结构化机器人策略。

Conclusion: 基础模型可以适配生成可解释的结构化机器人策略，为多模态机器人决策提供端到端不透明方法的替代方案，结合了高维学习和符号控制的优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in mapping multimodal observations to robot behaviors. However, most current approaches rely on end-to-end visuomotor policies that remain opaque and difficult to analyze, limiting their use in safety-critical robotic applications. In contrast, classical robotic systems often rely on structured policy representations that provide interpretability, modularity, and reactive execution. This work investigates how foundation models can be specialized to generate structured robot policies grounded in multimodal perception, bridging high-dimensional learning and symbolic control. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach in which a VLM synthesizes executable Behavior Tree policies from visual observations, natural language instructions, and structured system specifications. To enable scalable supervision without manual annotation, we introduce an automated pipeline that generates a synthetic multimodal dataset of domain-randomized scenes paired with instruction-policy examples produced by a foundation model. Real-world experiments on two robotic manipulators show that structured policies learned entirely from synthetic supervision transfer successfully to physical systems. The results indicate that foundation models can be adapted to produce interpretable and structured robot policies, providing an alternative to opaque end-to-end approaches for multimodal robot decision making.

</details>


### [123] [MFE: A Multimodal Hand Exoskeleton with Interactive Force, Pressure and Thermo-haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02820)
*Ziyuan Tang,Yitian Guo,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态反馈外骨骼(MFE)，整合力、压力、热反馈于一体，提升机器人遥操作系统的情境感知和可用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉设备通常只提供单模态反馈，无法提供包含力、压力、热感觉的丰富多模态感官反馈，限制了虚拟现实和机器人遥操作中的情境感知能力

Method: 设计多模态反馈外骨骼(MFE)：1) 20自由度手部姿态捕捉；2) 主动力反馈机制(3.5-8.1N推拉力)；3) 指尖电渗原理平面执行器(2.47kPa接触压力)；4) 热电热泵(10-55°C温度渲染)；集成到X-Arm 6和Inspire Hand机器人遥操作系统

Result: 用户研究中，参与者成功识别和操作可变形物体，区分不同温度的远程物体，证明MFE增强了情境感知、可用性和系统透明度

Conclusion: MFE通过整合多模态触觉反馈，有效提升了机器人遥操作系统的情境感知能力和用户体验，为虚拟现实和远程操作应用提供了更丰富的感官反馈解决方案

Abstract: Recent advancements in virtual reality and robotic teleoperation have greatly increased the variety of haptic information that must be conveyed to users. While existing haptic devices typically provide unimodal feedback to enhance situational awareness, a gap remains in their ability to deliver rich, multimodal sensory feedback encompassing force, pressure, and thermal sensations. To address this limitation, we present the Multimodal Feedback Exoskeleton (MFE), a hand exoskeleton designed to deliver hybrid haptic feedback. The MFE features 20 degrees of freedom for capturing hand pose. For force feedback, it employs an active mechanism capable of generating 3.5-8.1 N of pushing and pulling forces at the fingers' resting pose, enabling realistic interaction with deformable objects. The fingertips are equipped with flat actuators based on the electro-osmotic principle, providing pressure and vibration stimuli and achieving up to 2.47 kPa of contact pressure to render tactile sensations. For thermal feedback, the MFE integrates thermoelectric heat pumps capable of rendering temperatures from 10 to 55 degrees Celsius. We validated the MFE by integrating it into a robotic teleoperation system using the X-Arm 6 and Inspire Hand manipulator. In user studies, participants successfully recognized and manipulated deformable objects and differentiated remote objects with varying temperatures. These results demonstrate that the MFE enhances situational awareness, as well as the usability and transparency of robotic teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [124] [Goal-Conditioned Neural ODEs with Guaranteed Safety and Stability for Learning-Based All-Pairs Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02821)
*Dechuan Liu,Ruigang Wang,Ian R. Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于学习的方法解决任意起点-终点对运动规划问题，通过双Lipschitz微分同胚构建光滑的目标条件神经ODE，提供全局指数稳定性、安全性和收敛率等理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统运动规划方法在处理任意起点-终点对时的局限性，需要一种能够保证全局稳定性、安全性并提供理论保证的学习方法。

Method: 使用双Lipschitz微分同胚构建光滑的目标条件神经ODE，采用双Lipschitz神经网络实现可处理的学习实现，并可结合演示数据。

Result: 方法能保证无论目标位置如何都具有全局指数稳定性和安全性（安全集前向不变性），建立了收敛率、跟踪误差和向量场大小的显式边界。

Conclusion: 提出的学习框架为任意起点-终点对运动规划提供了理论保证和实际可行性，在2D走廊导航任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a learning-based approach for all-pairs motion planning, where the initial and goal states are allowed to be arbitrary points in a safe set. We construct smooth goal-conditioned neural ordinary differential equations (neural ODEs) via bi-Lipschitz diffeomorphisms. Theoretical results show that the proposed model can provide guarantees of global exponential stability and safety (safe set forward invariance) regardless of goal location. Moreover, explicit bounds on convergence rate, tracking error, and vector field magnitude are established. Our approach admits a tractable learning implementation using bi-Lipschitz neural networks and can incorporate demonstration data. We illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed method on a 2D corridor navigation task.

</details>


### [125] [Orientation Matters: Learning Radiation Patterns of Multi-Rotor UAVs In-Flight to Enhance Communication Availability Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02827)
*Martin Zoula,Daniel Bonilla Licea,Jan Faigl,Václav Navrátil,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过校准飞行数据学习异构四旋翼无人机天线辐射模式的方法，使用球谐函数或加权平均建模，通过多项式系数线性回归解耦两个独立无人机的辐射模式，在真实数据集上达到3.6dB RMS误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实应用中，无人机系统经常需要调整载荷，这会影响天线辐射模式。传统校准方法耗时且不灵活，需要一种快速重新校准的方法来支持精确的自主路径规划和集群控制。

Method: 使用校准飞行数据学习天线辐射模式，采用球谐函数级数或诱导样本加权平均进行建模。通过多项式系数线性回归同时解耦两个异构无人机的辐射模式，在无障碍消声高度设计联合校准轨迹以充分利用飞行时间。

Result: 在真实数据集上评估，成功学习到两个辐射模式，达到3.6 dB RMS误差，与测量噪声水平相当。证明该方法在载荷变化后能够实现快速重新校准。

Conclusion: 提出的辐射模式学习和解耦方法能够支持载荷变化后的快速重新校准，从而实现精确的自主路径规划和集群控制，适用于需要频繁调整设置的现实应用场景。

Abstract: The paper presents an approach for learning antenna Radiation Patterns (RPs) of a pair of heterogeneous quadrotor Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) by calibration flight data. RPs are modeled either as a Spherical Harmonics series or as a weighted average over inducing samples. Linear regression of polynomial coefficients simultaneously decouples the two independent UAVs' RPs. A joint calibration trajectory exploits available flight time in an obstacle-free anechoic altitude. Evaluation on a real-world dataset demonstrates the feasibility of learning both radiation patterns, achieving 3.6 dB RMS error, the measurement noise level. The proposed RP learning and decoupling can be exploited in rapid recalibration upon payload changes, thereby enabling precise autonomous path planning and swarm control in real-world applications where setup changes are expected.

</details>


### [126] [An Asynchronous Two-Speed Kalman Filter for Real-Time UUV Cooperative Navigation Under Acoustic Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02878)
*Shuyue Li,Miguel López-Benítez,Eng Gee Lim,Fei Ma,Qian Dong,Mengze Cao,Limin Yu,Xiaohui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异步双速卡尔曼滤波(TSKF)结合变分历史蒸馏(VHD)机制，解决水下GNSS拒止环境中UUV协同导航的通信延迟问题，实现控制、通信和计算的协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下GNSS拒止环境中，单个UUV存在无界航位推算漂移，需要协同导航。但水声信道通信延迟严重，传统滤波器(如EKF/UKF)要么阻塞控制循环等待延迟数据，要么丢弃延迟测量，导致严重漂移。

Method: 提出异步双速卡尔曼滤波(TSKF)架构，将估计过程解耦为两个并行线程：1)高速率线程使用高斯过程补偿的航位推算保证实时控制；2)低速率线程处理异步延迟的协同信息。引入有限长度状态缓冲区，将延迟测量应用于对应历史状态，并通过VHD投影将校正快速传递到当前时间。

Result: 仿真结果显示，TSKF在严重延迟(达30秒)下保持轨迹RMSE与计算密集的批量优化方法相当。执行时间在亚毫秒级，显著优于标准EKF/UKF。实现了有效的控制、通信和计算协同设计。

Conclusion: 提出的TSKF架构通过异步处理和VHD投影机制，有效解决了水下协同导航中的通信延迟问题，显著增强了自主海洋自动化系统的鲁棒性，实现了3C协同设计。

Abstract: In GNSS-denied underwater environments, individual unmanned underwater vehicles (UUVs) suffer from unbounded dead-reckoning drift, making collaborative navigation crucial for accurate state estimation. However, the severe communication delay inherent in underwater acoustic channels poses serious challenges to real-time state estimation. Traditional filters, such as Extended Kalman Filters (EKF) or Unscented Kalman Filters (UKF), usually block the main control loop while waiting for delayed data, or completely discard Out-of-Sequence Measurements (OOSM), resulting in serious drift. To address this, we propose an Asynchronous Two-Speed Kalman Filter (TSKF) enhanced by a novel projection mechanism, which we term Variational History Distillation (VHD). The proposed architecture decouples the estimation process into two parallel threads: a fast-rate thread that utilizes Gaussian Process (GP) compensated dead reckoning to guarantee high-frequency real-time control, and a slow-rate thread dedicated to processing asynchronously delayed collaborative information. By introducing a finite-length State Buffer, the algorithm applies delayed measurements (t-T) to their corresponding historical states, and utilizes a VHD-based projection to fast-forward the correction to the current time without computationally heavy recalculations. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed TSKF maintains trajectory Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) comparable to computationally intensive batch-optimization methods under severe delays (up to 30 s). Executing in sub-millisecond time, it significantly outperforms standard EKF/UKF. The results demonstrate an effective control, communication, and computing (3C) co-design that significantly enhances the resilience of autonomous marine automation systems.

</details>


### [127] [RAGE: A Tightly Coupled Radar-Aided Grip Estimator For Autonomous Race Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02892)
*Davide Malvezzi,Nicola Musiu,Eugenio Mascaro,Francesco Iacovacci,Marko Bertogna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAGE：一种仅使用标准传感器（IMU和雷达）实时估计车辆速度、轮胎侧偏角和侧向力的方法，适用于自动驾驶赛车在极限工况下的安全操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车需要在物理极限下安全运行，实时估计车辆-轮胎-路面摩擦至关重要。传统方法依赖昂贵、专用的传感器，安装复杂，限制了可扩展性和部署。

Method: 提出RAGE实时估计器，仅使用标准传感器（如IMU和雷达）同时推断车辆速度、轮胎侧偏角和侧向力。这些传感器在现代自动驾驶平台上普遍可用。

Result: 通过高保真仿真和EAV-24自动驾驶赛车的真实世界实验验证了该方法，证明了其在估计车辆侧向动力学方面的准确性和有效性。

Conclusion: RAGE提供了一种实用、可扩展的解决方案，使用标准传感器实时估计关键轮胎-路面摩擦参数，使自动驾驶赛车能够在极限工况下安全运行。

Abstract: Real-time estimation of vehicle-tire-road friction is critical for allowing autonomous race cars to safely and effectively operate at their physical limits. Traditional approaches to measure tire grip often depend on costly, specialized sensors that require custom installation, limiting scalability and deployment. In this work, we introduce RAGE, a novel real-time estimator that simultaneously infers the vehicle velocity, slip angles of the tires and the lateral forces that act on them, using only standard sensors, such as IMUs and RADARs, which are commonly available on most of modern autonomous platforms. We validate our approach through both high-fidelity simulations and real-world experiments conducted on the EAV-24 autonomous race car, demonstrating the accuracy and effectiveness of our method in estimating the vehicle lateral dynamics.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Task-Invariant Properties via Dreamer: Enabling Efficient Policy Transfer for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02911)
*Junyang Liang,Yuxuan Liu,Yabin Chang,Junfan Lin,Junkai Ji,Hui Li,Changxin Huang,Jianqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamTIP框架通过在大语言模型引导下学习任务不变属性，增强Dreamer世界模型的仿真到现实迁移能力，显著提升四足机器人在复杂地形上的运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在多样动态地形上的运动面临仿真与现实差异的挑战，传统仿真到现实迁移方法依赖人工特征设计或昂贵的真实世界微调，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出DreamTIP框架，在大语言模型引导下识别任务不变属性（如接触稳定性、地形间隙），将其作为辅助预测目标集成到Dreamer世界模型中，并设计混合回放缓冲区和正则化约束的高效适应策略。

Result: 在楼梯、攀爬、倾斜、爬行等复杂地形上，DreamTIP显著优于现有方法：在8个仿真迁移任务中平均性能提升28.1%，在真实世界攀爬任务中成功率从10%提升到100%。

Conclusion: 将任务不变属性融入Dreamer学习为机器人运动提供了一种新颖的鲁棒可迁移解决方案，有效解决了仿真到现实迁移的核心挑战。

Abstract: Achieving quadruped robot locomotion across diverse and dynamic terrains presents significant challenges, primarily due to the discrepancies between simulation environments and real-world conditions. Traditional sim-to-real transfer methods often rely on manual feature design or costly real-world fine-tuning. To address these limitations, this paper proposes the DreamTIP framework, which incorporates Task-Invariant Properties learning within the Dreamer world model architecture to enhance sim-to-real transfer capabilities. Guided by large language models, DreamTIP identifies and leverages Task-Invariant Properties, such as contact stability and terrain clearance, which exhibit robustness to dynamic variations and strong transferability across tasks. These properties are integrated into the world model as auxiliary prediction targets, enabling the policy to learn representations that are insensitive to underlying dynamic changes. Furthermore, an efficient adaptation strategy is designed, employing a mixed replay buffer and regularization constraints to rapidly calibrate to real-world dynamics while effectively mitigating representation collapse and catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments on complex terrains, including Stair, Climb, Tilt, and Crawl, demonstrate that DreamTIP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both simulated and real-world environments. Our method achieves an average performance improvement of 28.1% across eight distinct simulated transfer tasks. In the real-world Climb task, the baseline method achieved only a 10\ success rate, whereas our method attained a 100% success rate. These results indicate that incorporating Task-Invariant Properties into Dreamer learning offers a novel solution for achieving robust and transferable robot locomotion.

</details>


### [129] [Open-Loop Planning, Closed-Loop Verification: Speculative Verification for VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02965)
*Zihua Wang,Zhitao Lin,Ruibo Li,Yu Zhang,Xu Yang,Siya Mi,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SV-VLA框架结合了高效的开环长时程规划和轻量级闭环在线验证，在保持VLA模型效率的同时提升动态环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然性能强大但推理成本高，采用动作分块预测的方法虽然提高了效率，但开环执行对环境变化敏感且容易累积误差，缺乏闭环反馈。

Method: 提出SV-VLA框架：使用重型VLA作为低频宏观规划器生成动作块和规划上下文，同时使用轻量级验证器基于最新观察持续监控执行。验证器结合当前观察和规划上下文，比较规划动作与闭环参考动作，仅在必要时触发重新规划。

Result: 实验表明SV-VLA结合了分块预测的效率和闭环控制的鲁棒性，能够在动态环境中实现高效可靠的VLA控制。

Conclusion: SV-VLA框架成功解决了VLA模型在效率和鲁棒性之间的权衡问题，为动态环境中的具身控制提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, as large foundation models for embodied control, have shown strong performance in manipulation tasks. However, their performance comes at high inference cost. To improve efficiency, recent methods adopt action chunking, which predicts a sequence of future actions for open-loop execution. Although effective for reducing computation, open-loop execution is sensitive to environmental changes and prone to error accumulation due to the lack of close-loop feedback. To address this limitation, we propose Speculative Verification for VLA Control (SV-VLA), a framework that combines efficient open-loop long-horizon planning with lightweight closed-loop online verification. Specifically, SV-VLA uses a heavy VLA as a low-frequency macro-planner to generate an action chunk together with a planning context, while a lightweight verifier continuously monitors execution based on the latest observations. Conditioned on both the current observation and the planning context, the verifier compares the planned action against a closed-loop reference action and triggers replanning only when necessary. Experiments demonstrate that SV-VLA combines the efficiency of chunked prediction with the robustness of closed-loop control, enabling efficient and reliable VLA-based control in dynamic environments. Code is available: https://github.com/edsad122/SV-VLA.

</details>


### [130] [A Flow Matching Framework for Soft-Robot Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03006)
*Hang Yang,Fangju Yang,Yangming Zhang,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Zhenye Luo,Zixi Chen,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于流匹配的软体连续机器人逆动力学学习框架，将逆动力学重构为条件流匹配问题，使用Rectified Flow生成物理一致的控制输入，相比传统回归方法将轨迹跟踪RMSE降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续机器人的逆动力学学习面临高维非线性和复杂驱动耦合的挑战。传统反馈控制器存在控制抖动问题，而确定性回归方法难以捕捉复杂的非线性映射以实现精确的动态跟踪。

Method: 提出开环前馈控制的逆动力学框架，将逆动力学重构为条件流匹配问题，采用Rectified Flow作为轻量级实例生成物理一致的控制输入而非条件平均值。引入两个变体：RF-Physical使用基于物理的先验进行残差建模；RF-FWD在流匹配中集成前向动力学一致性损失。

Result: 框架将轨迹跟踪RMSE降低超过50%（相比MLP、LSTM、Transformer等基准方法）。系统在1.14 m/s的峰值末端执行器速度下保持稳定开环执行，推理延迟低于1毫秒（0.995 ms）。

Conclusion: 流匹配是学习软体机器人系统微分逆动力学的鲁棒高性能范式，能够生成物理一致的控制输入，显著提升跟踪精度和实时性能。

Abstract: Learning the inverse dynamics of soft continuum robots remains challenging due to high-dimensional nonlinearities and complex actuation coupling. Conventional feedback-based controllers often suffer from control chattering due to corrective oscillations, while deterministic regression-based learners struggle to capture the complex nonlinear mappings required for accurate dynamic tracking. Motivated by these limitations, we propose an inverse-dynamics framework for open-loop feedforward control that learns the system's differential dynamics as a generative transport map. Specifically, inverse dynamics is reformulated as a conditional flow-matching problem, and Rectified Flow (RF) is adopted as a lightweight instance to generate physically consistent control inputs rather than conditional averages. Two variants are introduced to further enhance physical consistency: RF-Physical, utilizing a physics-based prior for residual modeling; and RF-FWD, integrating a forward-dynamics consistency loss during flow matching. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework reduces trajectory tracking RMSE by over 50% compared to standard regression baselines (MLP, LSTM, Transformer). The system sustains stable open-loop execution at a peak end-effector velocity of 1.14 m/s with sub-millisecond inference latency (0.995 ms). This work demonstrates flow matching as a robust, high-performance paradigm for learning differential inverse dynamics in soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [131] [Asymptotically-Bounded 3D Frontier Exploration enhanced with Bayesian Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03008)
*John Lewis,Meysam Basiri,Pedro U. Lima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于OctoMap的边界探索算法，具有可预测的渐近性能边界，复杂度仅与边界数量相关，而非环境大小，并通过贝叶斯回归器提升信息增益估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模环境中的机器人探索计算成本高，传统方法复杂度随环境规模增长，需要更高效、可预测性能的探索算法。

Method: 1) 基于OctoMap的边界探索算法，复杂度O(|F|)；2) 战略性的前向和逆向传感器建模实现近似但高效的边界检测与维护；3) 集成贝叶斯回归器估计信息增益，避免显式计数未知体素。

Result: 仿真显示比现有OctoMap方法计算效率更高，与不依赖OctoMap的基线方法相当；贝叶斯增强框架相比确定性边界基线在总探索时间上提升达54%；真实实验验证了计算边界和增强效果。

Conclusion: 提出的算法在大规模环境中实现了可预测的渐近性能边界，显著提升了计算效率，同时保证任务完成，适用于实际机器人探索应用。

Abstract: Robotic exploration in large-scale environments is computationally demanding due to the high overhead of processing extensive frontiers. This article presents an OctoMap-based frontier exploration algorithm with predictable, asymptotically bounded performance. Unlike conventional methods whose complexity scales with environment size, our approach maintains a complexity of $\mathcal{O}(|\mathcal{F}|)$, where $|\mathcal{F}|$ is the number of frontiers. This is achieved through strategic forward and inverse sensor modeling, which enables approximate yet efficient frontier detection and maintenance. To further enhance performance, we integrate a Bayesian regressor to estimate information gain, circumventing the need to explicitly count unknown voxels when prioritizing viewpoints. Simulations show the proposed method is more computationally efficient than the existing OctoMap-based methods and achieves computational efficiency comparable to baselines that are independent of OctoMap. Specifically, the Bayesian-enhanced framework achieves up to a $54\%$ improvement in total exploration time compared to standard deterministic frontier-based baselines across varying spatial scales, while guaranteeing task completion. Real-world experiments confirm the computational bounds as well as the effectiveness of the proposed enhancement.

</details>


### [132] [Behavior-Constrained Reinforcement Learning with Receding-Horizon Credit Assignment for High-Performance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03023)
*Siwei Ju,Jan Tauberschmidt,Oleg Arenz,Peter van Vliet,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出行为约束强化学习框架，通过轨迹级约束和前瞻奖励机制，在赛车仿真中学习高性能且与专家行为一致的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习可能偏离期望的人类行为，而模仿学习受限于演示质量且难以超越专家数据。需要一种既能超越演示性能又能控制与专家行为偏差的方法。

Method: 提出行为约束强化学习框架，引入滚动时域预测机制建模短期未来轨迹并提供前瞻奖励；使用参考轨迹条件化策略，表示专家一致行为的分布而非单一确定性目标。

Result: 在高保真赛车仿真中使用专业车手数据评估，学习到的策略在保持与专家驾驶行为紧密对齐的同时获得有竞争力的圈速，在性能和模仿质量上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够学习既高性能又行为一致的控制策略，可作为复杂控制系统中人类决策的可靠替代，在驾驶员在环仿真中再现了专业车手的驾驶特性。

Abstract: Learning high-performance control policies that remain consistent with expert behavior is a fundamental challenge in robotics. Reinforcement learning can discover high-performing strategies but often departs from desirable human behavior, whereas imitation learning is limited by demonstration quality and struggles to improve beyond expert data. We propose a behavior-constrained reinforcement learning framework that improves beyond demonstrations while explicitly controlling deviation from expert behavior. Because expert-consistent behavior in dynamic control is inherently trajectory-level, we introduce a receding-horizon predictive mechanism that models short-term future trajectories and provides look-ahead rewards during training. To account for the natural variability of human behavior under disturbances and changing conditions, we further condition the policy on reference trajectories, allowing it to represent a distribution of expert-consistent behaviors rather than a single deterministic target. Empirically, we evaluate the approach in high-fidelity race car simulation using data from professional drivers, a domain characterized by extreme dynamics and narrow performance margins. The learned policies achieve competitive lap times while maintaining close alignment with expert driving behavior, outperforming baseline methods in both performance and imitation quality. Beyond standard benchmarks, we conduct human-grounded evaluation in a driver-in-the-loop simulator and show that the learned policies reproduce setup-dependent driving characteristics consistent with the feedback of top-class professional race drivers. These results demonstrate that our method enables learning high-performance control policies that are both optimal and behavior-consistent, and can serve as reliable surrogates for human decision-making in complex control systems.

</details>


### [133] [ARM: Advantage Reward Modeling for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03037)
*Yiming Mao,Zixi Yu,Weixin Mao,Yinhao Li,Qirui Hu,Zihan Lan,Minzhao Zhu,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARM框架通过相对优势估计替代稀疏奖励，使用三状态标注策略减少人工标注成本，在长时程机器人操作任务中实现99.4%成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作中稀疏奖励难以指导信用分配，而密集进度奖励成本高且不适合非单调行为（如回溯和恢复）

Method: 提出优势奖励建模（ARM）框架，从绝对进度转向相对优势估计；引入三状态标注策略（进步、退步、停滞）减少人工认知负担；训练模型实现自动进度标注；集成到离线RL管道中进行自适应动作-奖励重加权

Result: 在挑战性长时程毛巾折叠任务中达到99.4%成功率，相比当前VLA基线提高了稳定性和数据效率，策略训练期间几乎无需人工干预

Conclusion: ARM框架通过相对优势估计和高效标注策略，有效解决了长时程机器人操作中的奖励稀疏问题，实现了高性能且数据高效的策略学习

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation remains challenging for reinforcement learning (RL) because sparse rewards provide limited guidance for credit assignment. Practical policy improvement thus relies on richer intermediate supervision, such as dense progress rewards, which are costly to obtain and ill-suited to non-monotonic behaviors such as backtracking and recovery. To address this, we propose Advantage Reward Modeling (ARM), a framework that shifts from hard-to-quantify absolute progress to estimating relative advantage. We introduce a cost-effective tri-state labeling strategy -- Progressive, Regressive, and Stagnant -- that reduces human cognitive overhead while ensuring high cross-annotator consistency. By training on these intuitive signals, ARM enables automated progress annotation for both complete demonstrations and fragmented DAgger-style data. Integrating ARM into an offline RL pipeline allows for adaptive action-reward reweighting, effectively filtering suboptimal samples. Our approach achieves a 99.4% success rate on a challenging long-horizon towel-folding task, demonstrating improved stability and data efficiency over current VLA baselines with near-zero human intervention during policy training.

</details>


### [134] [Enhancing Multi-Robot Exploration Using Probabilistic Frontier Prioritization with Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03042)
*John Lewis Devassy,Meysam Basiri,Mário A. T. Figueiredo,Pedro U. Lima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于概率前沿优先级的增强方法，集成到现有多智能体探索算法中，在通信受限环境下提升探索性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体自主探索在环境监测、搜救等应用中很重要，但通信约束下的有效协调仍是挑战。现有前沿探索算法通过分析已知与未知区域边界来确定最佳观测点，但需要改进前沿优先级排序质量。

Method: 引入概率前沿优先级方法，使用狄利克雷过程高斯混合模型(DP-GMM)和信息增益的概率公式化，增强前沿优先级的质量。该方法集成到两种最先进的多智能体探索算法中。

Result: 在不同杂乱程度、通信约束和团队规模的环境中，性能持续提升。模拟显示两种算法在所有组合下平均增益分别为10%和14%。双无人机系统的真实世界实验成功验证了结果。

Conclusion: 提出的概率前沿优先级增强方法能有效提升多智能体探索算法的性能，特别是在通信受限环境下，通过改进前沿选择质量实现更高效的探索。

Abstract: Multi-agent autonomous exploration is essential for applications such as environmental monitoring, search and rescue, and industrial-scale surveillance. However, effective coordination under communication constraints remains a significant challenge. Frontier exploration algorithms analyze the boundary between the known and unknown regions to determine the next-best view that maximizes exploratory gain. This article proposes an enhancement to existing frontier-based exploration algorithms by introducing a probabilistic approach to frontier prioritization. By leveraging Dirichlet process Gaussian mixture model (DP-GMM) and a probabilistic formulation of information gain, the method improves the quality of frontier prioritization. The proposed enhancement, integrated into two state-of-the-art multi-agent exploration algorithms, consistently improves performance across environments of varying clutter, communication constraints, and team sizes. Simulations showcase an average gain of $10\%$ and $14\%$ for the two algorithms across all combinations. Successful deployment in real-world experiments with a dual-drone system further corroborates these findings.

</details>


### [135] [Joint Prediction of Human Motions and Actions in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03065)
*Alessandra Bulanti,Alessandro Carfì,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MA-HERP：一个用于联合估计和预测人类动作与运动的层次递归概率框架，结合连续动态、离散标签和持续时间，支持人机协作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流畅的人机协作需要机器人持续估计人类行为并预测未来意图，这需要联合推理连续运动和离散动作，而目前这两者大多被孤立建模

Method: 提出MA-HERP框架，包含：(1) 层次表示：通过Allen区间关系将运动组合成动作；(2) 统一概率分解：耦合连续动态、离散标签和持续时间；(3) 递归推理方案：受贝叶斯滤波启发，交替进行自上而下的动作预测和自下而上的感官证据处理

Result: 基于肌肉骨骼模拟的神经模型初步实验显示：准确的运动预测、噪声下的鲁棒动作推断，以及与人机在线协作兼容的计算性能

Conclusion: MA-HERP为联合估计和预测人类动作与运动提供了一个有效的概率框架，支持流畅的人机协作

Abstract: Fluent human--robot collaboration requires robots to continuously estimate human behaviour and anticipate future intentions. This entails reasoning jointly about \emph{continuous movements} and \emph{discrete actions}, which are still largely modelled in isolation. In this paper, we introduce \textsf{MA-HERP}, a hierarchical and recursive probabilistic framework for the \emph{joint estimation and prediction} of human movements and actions. The model combines: (i) a hierarchical representation in which movements compose into actions through admissible Allen interval relations, (ii) a unified probabilistic factorisation coupling continuous dynamics, discrete labels, and durations, and (iii) a recursive inference scheme inspired by Bayesian filtering, alternating top-down action prediction with bottom-up sensory evidence. We present a preliminary experimental evaluation based on neural models trained on musculoskeletal simulations of reaching movements, showing accurate motion prediction, robust action inference under noise, and computational performance compatible with on-line human--robot collaboration.

</details>


### [136] [Flash-Mono: Feed-Forward Accelerated Gaussian Splatting Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03092)
*Zicheng Zhang,Ke Wu,Xiangting Meng,Keyu Liu,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Flash-Mono：一种基于前馈预测的单目3D高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过多帧上下文直接预测高斯属性，实现10倍加速，并利用2D高斯面元和隐藏状态环闭模块提升几何精度与多视图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目3D高斯溅射SLAM存在时间效率低、几何精度差和多视图不一致的问题，主要源于每帧从头训练优化的耗时以及单帧几何先验缺乏帧间尺度一致性。需要一种利用多帧上下文直接预测高斯属性的前馈范式来解决这些挑战。

Method: 系统包含三个核心模块：1）前馈预测前端：训练循环前馈模型，通过交叉注意力渐进聚合多帧视觉特征到隐藏状态，联合预测相机位姿和逐像素高斯属性；2）2D高斯溅射映射后端：用2D高斯面元替代传统3D高斯椭球体提升几何保真度；3）高效隐藏状态环闭模块：利用隐藏状态作为紧凑子图描述符，实现环闭检测和全局Sim(3)优化以消除漂移。

Result: 相比基于优化的GS-SLAM实现10倍加速，同时确保高质量渲染。在跟踪和建图质量上达到最先进性能，有效解决了漂移问题，提升了时间效率和几何精度。

Conclusion: Flash-Mono通过前馈预测范式成功解决了单目3D高斯溅射SLAM的关键限制，在效率、精度和一致性方面均有显著提升，展现了在具身感知和实时重建应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM suffers from critical limitations in time efficiency, geometric accuracy, and multi-view consistency. These issues stem from the time-consuming $\textit{Train-from-Scratch}$ optimization and the lack of inter-frame scale consistency from single-frame geometry priors. We contend that a feed-forward paradigm, leveraging multi-frame context to predict Gaussian attributes directly, is crucial for addressing these challenges. We present Flash-Mono, a system composed of three core modules: a feed-forward prediction frontend, a 2D Gaussian Splatting mapping backend, and an efficient hidden-state-based loop closure module. We trained a recurrent feed-forward frontend model that progressively aggregates multi-frame visual features into a hidden state via cross attention and jointly predicts camera poses and per-pixel Gaussian properties. By directly predicting Gaussian attributes, our method bypasses the burdensome per-frame optimization required in optimization-based GS-SLAM, achieving a $\textbf{10x}$ speedup while ensuring high-quality rendering. The power of our recurrent architecture extends beyond efficient prediction. The hidden states act as compact submap descriptors, facilitating efficient loop closure and global $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ optimization to mitigate the long-standing challenge of drift. For enhanced geometric fidelity, we replace conventional 3D Gaussian ellipsoids with 2D Gaussian surfels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Flash-Mono achieves state-of-the-art performance in both tracking and mapping quality, highlighting its potential for embodied perception and real-time reconstruction applications. Project page: https://victkk.github.io/flash-mono.

</details>


### [137] [An Open-Source LiDAR and Monocular Off-Road Autonomous Navigation Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03096)
*Rémi Marsal,Quentin Picard,Adrien Poiré,Sébastien Kerbourc'h,Thibault Toralba,Clément Yver,Alexandre Chapoutot,David Filliat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个开源越野导航系统，支持LiDAR和单目3D感知，无需任务特定训练，使用Depth Anything V2和VINS-Mono实现单目深度估计，在模拟和真实环境中性能接近高分辨率LiDAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自主导航需要可靠的3D感知，但LiDAR成本高、功耗大。基于基础模型的单目深度估计提供了轻量级替代方案，但其在户外导航系统中的集成尚未充分探索。

Method: 结合零样本深度预测（Depth Anything V2）和稀疏SLAM测量（VINS-Mono）进行度量深度重缩放。采用边缘掩码减少障碍物幻觉，时间平滑减轻SLAM不稳定性影响。生成机器人中心2.5D高程图用于基于成本地图的规划。

Result: 在逼真模拟（Isaac Sim）和真实世界非结构化环境中评估，单目配置在大多数场景中匹配高分辨率LiDAR性能，证明基于基础模型的单目深度估计是越野导航的可行LiDAR替代方案。

Conclusion: 开源导航栈和仿真环境提供了一个完整的越野导航管道和可复现基准，展示了基础模型单目深度估计在越野导航中的实用性和可行性。

Abstract: Off-road autonomous navigation demands reliable 3D perception for robust obstacle detection in challenging unstructured terrain. While LiDAR is accurate, it is costly and power-intensive. Monocular depth estimation using foundation models offers a lightweight alternative, but its integration into outdoor navigation stacks remains underexplored. We present an open-source off-road navigation stack supporting both LiDAR and monocular 3D perception without task-specific training. For the monocular setup, we combine zero-shot depth prediction (Depth Anything V2) with metric depth rescaling using sparse SLAM measurements (VINS-Mono). Two key enhancements improve robustness: edge-masking to reduce obstacle hallucination and temporal smoothing to mitigate the impact of SLAM instability. The resulting point cloud is used to generate a robot-centric 2.5D elevation map for costmap-based planning. Evaluated in photorealistic simulations (Isaac Sim) and real-world unstructured environments, the monocular configuration matches high-resolution LiDAR performance in most scenarios, demonstrating that foundation-model-based monocular depth estimation is a viable LiDAR alternative for robust off-road navigation. By open-sourcing the navigation stack and the simulation environment, we provide a complete pipeline for off-road navigation as well as a reproducible benchmark. Code available at https://github.com/LARIAD/Offroad-Nav.

</details>


### [138] [FSUNav: A Cerebrum-Cerebellum Architecture for Fast, Safe, and Universal Zero-Shot Goal-Oriented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03139)
*Mingao Tan,Yiyang Li,Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FSUNav提出脑-小脑架构，结合视觉语言模型与强化学习，实现跨异构机器人的快速、安全、零样本目标导航，支持多模态输入和开放词汇语义泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言导航方法存在异构机器人兼容性差、实时性能不足、导航安全性低等问题，且难以支持开放词汇语义泛化和多模态任务输入，需要更通用、安全、高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用脑-小脑架构：小脑模块基于深度强化学习开发通用局部规划器，实现跨异构平台统一导航；大脑模块构建三层推理模型，利用视觉语言模型建立端到端检测验证机制，支持零样本开放词汇目标导航和多模态输入。

Result: 在MP3D、HM3D和OVON基准测试中，FSUNav在物体、实例图像和任务导航方面达到最先进性能，显著优于现有方法。在多种机器人平台上的实际部署验证了其鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: FSUNav通过创新的脑-小脑架构成功解决了异构机器人兼容性、实时性能、导航安全性等瓶颈问题，实现了快速、安全、通用的零样本目标导航，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Current vision-language navigation methods face substantial bottlenecks regarding heterogeneous robot compatibility, real-time performance, and navigation safety. Furthermore, they struggle to support open-vocabulary semantic generalization and multimodal task inputs. To address these challenges, this paper proposes FSUNav: a Cerebrum-Cerebellum architecture for fast, safe, and universal zero-shot goal-oriented navigation, which innovatively integrates vision-language models (VLMs) with the proposed architecture. The cerebellum module, a high-frequency end-to-end module, develops a universal local planner based on deep reinforcement learning, enabling unified navigation across heterogeneous platforms (e.g., humanoid, quadruped, wheeled robots) to improve navigation efficiency while significantly reducing collision risk. The cerebrum module constructs a three-layer reasoning model and leverages VLMs to build an end-to-end detection and verification mechanism, enabling zero-shot open-vocabulary goal navigation without predefined IDs and improving task success rates in both simulation and real-world environments. Additionally, the framework supports multimodal inputs (e.g., text, target descriptions, and images), further enhancing generalization, real-time performance, safety, and robustness. Experimental results on MP3D, HM3D, and OVON benchmarks demonstrate that FSUNav achieves state-of-the-art performance on object, instance image, and task navigation, significantly outperforming existing methods. Real-world deployments on diverse robotic platforms further validate its robustness and practical applicability.

</details>


### [139] [Multi-View Video Diffusion Policy: A 3D Spatio-Temporal-Aware Video Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03181)
*Peiyan Li,Yixiang Chen,Yuan Xu,Jiabing Yang,Xiangnan Wu,Jun Guo,Nan Sun,Long Qian,Xinghang Li,Xin Xiao,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Tao Kong,Yan Huang,Liang Wang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MV-VDP：一种多视角视频扩散策略，通过联合建模3D时空环境状态，实现数据高效、鲁棒、可泛化的机器人操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略通常依赖2D视觉观察和静态图像-文本对预训练，忽视了3D空间结构和时间动态，导致数据需求高且对环境动态理解有限

Method: 提出多视角视频扩散策略，同时预测多视角热图视频和RGB视频，将视频预训练表示格式与动作微调对齐，并预测环境在机器人动作下的演化

Result: 仅需10条演示轨迹且无需额外预训练，MV-VDP能成功执行复杂真实世界任务，在模型超参数范围内表现鲁棒，能泛化到分布外设置，并预测真实未来视频

Conclusion: MV-VDP在Meta-World和真实机器人平台上持续优于基于视频预测、3D基础和视觉-语言-动作模型，为数据高效多任务操作建立了新的最先进水平

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires understanding both the 3D spatial structure of the environment and its temporal evolution, yet most existing policies overlook one or both. They typically rely on 2D visual observations and backbones pretrained on static image--text pairs, resulting in high data requirements and limited understanding of environment dynamics. To address this, we introduce MV-VDP, a multi-view video diffusion policy that jointly models the 3D spatio-temporal state of the environment. The core idea is to simultaneously predict multi-view heatmap videos and RGB videos, which 1) align the representation format of video pretraining with action finetuning, and 2) specify not only what actions the robot should take, but also how the environment is expected to evolve in response to those actions. Extensive experiments show that MV-VDP enables data-efficient, robust, generalizable, and interpretable manipulation. With only ten demonstration trajectories and without additional pretraining, MV-VDP successfully performs complex real-world tasks, demonstrates strong robustness across a range of model hyperparameters, generalizes to out-of-distribution settings, and predicts realistic future videos. Experiments on Meta-World and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that MV-VDP consistently outperforms video-prediction--based, 3D-based, and vision--language--action models, establishing a new state of the art in data-efficient multi-task manipulation.

</details>


### [140] [The Compression Gap: Why Discrete Tokenization Limits Vision-Language-Action Model Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03191)
*Takuya Shiba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现：在视觉-语言-动作模型中，当动作表示为离散token时，升级视觉编码器不会提升下游操作性能，因为固定容量的codebook成为信息瓶颈；而连续动作表示（如Diffusion Policy）则能从编码器升级中受益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为升级视觉编码器应该像在视觉-语言模型中一样提升视觉-语言-动作模型的性能，但作者发现当动作表示为离散token时，这种预期并不成立，需要探究其原因。

Method: 提出"压缩差距"信息论原理，通过LIBERO基准测试进行三方面验证：1）因子实验比较不同模型规模下编码器升级的影响；2）编码器质量梯度测试；3）codebook容量实验。

Result: Diffusion Policy（连续动作）从编码器升级中获益超过21个百分点，而OAT（离散动作）的增益显著衰减；编码器质量梯度测试显示Diffusion Policy性能随编码器质量单调提升，OAT保持平坦；增加codebook容量能部分恢复编码器敏感性。

Conclusion: 在物理AI中，扩展性能需要识别管道中的信息瓶颈位置，而不是简单地增加模型或数据规模。当动作离散化时，固定容量的codebook成为约束瓶颈，阻碍上游编码器改进的传播。

Abstract: Scaling Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by upgrading the vision encoder is expected to improve downstream manipulation performance--as it does in vision-language modeling. We show that this expectation fails when actions are represented as discrete tokens, and explain why through an information-theoretic principle we call the Compression Gap: in any visuomotor pipeline, scaling behavior is governed by the location of the tightest information bottleneck. When actions are continuous (e.g., Diffusion Policy), the vision encoder is the binding constraint, and upgrading it directly improves performance. When actions are discretized through a fixed-capacity codebook (e.g., OAT), the codebook becomes the binding constraint, and encoder improvements cannot propagate past it--regardless of how rich the upstream representation is. We validate this principle on the LIBERO benchmark with three lines of evidence: a factorial experiment showing that encoder upgrades improve Diffusion Policy by over 21 percentage points while OAT gains are substantially attenuated across model scales; an encoder quality gradient across four encoders confirming that Diffusion Policy tracks encoder quality monotonically while OAT remains flat; and a codebook size experiment demonstrating that relaxing codebook capacity partially recovers encoder sensitivity, providing causal evidence for the bottleneck hypothesis. Our findings reveal that scaling in Physical AI requires identifying where information bottlenecks lie in the pipeline, rather than uniformly increasing model or data size.

</details>


### [141] [Safety-Critical Centralized Nonlinear MPC for Cooperative Payload Transportation by Two Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03200)
*Ruturaj S. Sambhus,Yicheng Zeng,Kapi Ketan Mehta,Jeeseop Kim,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于双四足机器人协同运输的安全关键集中式非线性模型预测控制框架，通过控制屏障函数确保避障安全，并在真实硬件实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多四足机器人协同运输复杂载荷时的安全控制问题，特别是在存在环境障碍、质量惯性不确定性和外部干扰的情况下，需要确保机器人及载荷的避障安全。

Method: 采用离散时间非线性微分代数系统建模机器人-载荷耦合动力学，开发基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的NMPC框架，将交互力作为决策变量，形成结构化DAE约束最优控制问题，实现实时高效计算。

Result: 在两个Unitree Go2平台上进行了大量硬件实验，验证了算法在复杂环境中协同运输的有效性，能够处理质量惯性不确定性和外部推力干扰。

Conclusion: 提出的安全关键NMPC框架成功实现了双四足机器人的协同载荷运输，在复杂环境下确保了避障安全，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a safety-critical centralized nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework for cooperative payload transportation by two quadrupedal robots. The interconnected robot-payload system is modeled as a discrete-time nonlinear differential-algebraic system, capturing the coupled dynamics through holonomic constraints and interaction wrenches. To ensure safety in complex environments, we develop a control barrier function (CBF)-based NMPC formulation that enforces collision avoidance constraints for both the robots and the payload. The proposed approach retains the interaction wrenches as decision variables, resulting in a structured DAE-constrained optimal control problem that enables efficient real-time implementation. The effectiveness of the algorithm is validated through extensive hardware experiments on two Unitree Go2 platforms performing cooperative payload transportation in cluttered environments under mass and inertia uncertainty and external push disturbances.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Holos: A Web-Scale LLM-Based Multi-Agent System for the Agentic Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02334)
*Xiaohang Nie,Zihan Guo,Zicai Cui,Jiachi Yang,Zeyi Chen,Leheyi De,Yu Zhang,Junwei Liao,Bo Huang,Yingxuan Yang,Zhi Han,Zimian Peng,Linyao Chen,Wenzheng Tom Tang,Zongkai Liu,Tao Zhou,Botao Amber Hu,Shuyang Tang,Jianghao Lin,Weiwen Liu,Muning Wen,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Holos是一个为长期生态持久性设计的Web规模LLM驱动多智能体系统，采用五层架构解决开放世界中的扩展摩擦、协调崩溃和价值耗散问题，旨在为下一代自组织和持续演化的智能体网络奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的智能体从孤立任务解决者转变为持久数字实体，智能体网络（Agentic Web）的出现标志着向AGI的关键转变。然而，基于LLM的多智能体系统面临开放世界问题，包括扩展摩擦、协调崩溃和价值耗散等挑战。

Method: Holos采用五层架构，核心模块包括：1) Nuwa引擎用于高效智能体生成和托管；2) 市场驱动的协调器（Orchestrator）实现弹性协调；3) 内生价值循环实现激励兼容性。该系统旨在弥合微观协作与宏观涌现之间的差距。

Result: 研究团队已公开发布Holos系统（可通过https://holosai.io访问），为社区提供资源，并为大规模智能体生态系统未来研究提供测试平台。

Conclusion: Holos通过其架构设计解决了LLM多智能体系统的开放世界问题，为下一代自组织和持续演化的智能体网络奠定了基础，有望推动大规模智能体生态系统的发展。

Abstract: As large language models (LLM)-driven agents transition from isolated task solvers to persistent digital entities, the emergence of the Agentic Web, an ecosystem where heterogeneous agents autonomously interact and co-evolve, marks a pivotal shift toward Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). However, LLM-based multi-agent systems (LaMAS) are hindered by open-world issues such as scaling friction, coordination breakdown, and value dissipation. To address these challenges, we introduce Holos, a web-scale LaMAS architected for long-term ecological persistence. Holos adopts a five-layer architecture, with core modules primarily featuring the Nuwa engine for high-efficiency agent generation and hosting, a market-driven Orchestrator for resilient coordination, and an endogenous value cycle to achieve incentive compatibility. By bridging the gap between micro-level collaboration and macro-scale emergence, Holos hopes to lay the foundation for the next generation of the self-organizing and continuously evolving Agentic Web. We have publicly released Holos (accessible at https://holosai.io), providing a resource for the community and a testbed for future research in large-scale agentic ecosystems.

</details>


### [143] [Xpertbench: Expert Level Tasks with Rubrics-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02368)
*Xue Liu,Xin Ma,Yuxin Ma,Yongchang Peng,Duo Wang,Zhoufutu Wen,Ge Zhang,Kaiyuan Zhang,Xinyu Chen,Tianci He,Jiani Hou,Liang Hu,Ziyun Huang,Yongzhe Hui,Jianpeng Jiao,Chennan Ju,Yingru Kong,Yiran Li,Mengyun Liu,Luyao Ma,Fei Ni,Yiqing Ni,Yueyan Qiu,Yanle Ren,Zilin Shi,Zaiyuan Wang,Wenjie Yue,Shiyu Zhang,Xinyi Zhang,Kaiwen Zhao,Zhenwei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XpertBench是一个评估大语言模型在真实专业领域能力的高保真基准，包含1,346个专家设计的任务，采用ShotJudge评估范式，发现当前顶尖模型在专家级任务上存在显著性能天花板（最高约66%成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架存在领域覆盖窄、依赖通用任务或自评估偏差等问题，无法有效评估LLMs在复杂开放任务中的真实专家级认知能力。需要建立一个能反映真实专业场景的高保真基准。

Method: 构建XpertBench基准：包含1,346个专家精心设计的任务，覆盖金融、医疗、法律、教育、STEM和人文等80个类别；引入ShotJudge评估范式，使用经过专家few-shot示例校准的LLM评估器来减少自奖励偏差。

Result: 当前最先进的LLMs在专家级任务上表现出显著性能天花板：顶尖模型最高成功率仅约66%，平均得分约55%；模型在不同领域表现出非重叠优势（定量推理vs语言合成），揭示了显著的"专家差距"。

Conclusion: XpertBench揭示了当前AI系统在专家级能力上的显著差距，为从通用助手向专业协作伙伴的过渡提供了关键评估工具，强调了需要针对专业领域进行更深入的能力开发。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit plateauing performance on conventional benchmarks, a pivotal challenge persists: evaluating their proficiency in complex, open-ended tasks characterizing genuine expert-level cognition. Existing frameworks suffer from narrow domain coverage, reliance on generalist tasks, or self-evaluation biases. To bridge this gap, we present XpertBench, a high-fidelity benchmark engineered to assess LLMs across authentic professional domains. XpertBench consists of 1,346 meticulously curated tasks across 80 categories, spanning finance, healthcare, legal services, education, and dual-track research (STEM and Humanities). These tasks are derived from over 1,000 submissions by domain experts--including researchers from elite institutions and practitioners with extensive clinical or industrial experience--ensuring superior ecological validity. Each task uses detailed rubrics with mostly 15-40 weighted checkpoints to assess professional rigor. To facilitate scalable yet human-aligned assessment, we introduce ShotJudge, a novel evaluation paradigm that employs LLM judges calibrated with expert few-shot exemplars to mitigate self-rewarding biases. Our empirical evaluation of state-of-the-art LLMs reveals a pronounced performance ceiling: even leading models achieve a peak success rate of only ~66%, with a mean score around 55%. Models also exhibit domain-specific divergence, showing non-overlapping strengths in quantitative reasoning versus linguistic synthesis.. These findings underscore a significant "expert-gap" in current AI systems and establish XpertBench as a critical instrument for navigating the transition from general-purpose assistants to specialized professional collaborators.

</details>


### [144] [Compositional Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02434)
*Anugyan Das,Omkar Ghugarkar,Vishvesh Bhat,Asad Aali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，通过提取网格中的对象级结构、使用神经先验从领域特定语言中提出候选变换，并利用跨示例一致性过滤假设，来改进抽象推理语料库（ARC）的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯神经架构缺乏可靠组合泛化能力，而纯符号系统难以处理感知基础问题。需要结合两者优势来解决ARC中的抽象推理任务。

Method: 提出神经符号架构：1）从网格中提取对象级结构；2）使用神经先验从固定领域特定语言（DSL）中提出候选原子模式变换；3）利用跨示例一致性过滤假设。该框架基于人类视觉抽象启发的单元模式，为大型语言模型提供对象表示和变换建议。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2上，将基础LLM性能从16%提升到24.4%（公开评估集），与ARC Lang Solver结合后达到30.8%。

Conclusion: 分离感知、神经引导的变换提议和符号一致性过滤，无需任务特定微调或强化学习即可改善泛化能力，同时减少对暴力搜索和基于采样的测试时扩展的依赖。

Abstract: We study structured abstraction-based reasoning for the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) and compare its generalization to test-time approaches. Purely neural architectures lack reliable combinatorial generalization, while strictly symbolic systems struggle with perceptual grounding. We therefore propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that extracts object-level structure from grids, uses neural priors to propose candidate transformations from a fixed domain-specific language (DSL) of atomic patterns, and filters hypotheses using cross-example consistency. Instantiated as a compositional reasoning framework based on unit patterns inspired by human visual abstraction, the system augments large language models (LLMs) with object representations and transformation proposals. On ARC-AGI-2, it improves base LLM performance from 16% to 24.4% on the public evaluation set, and to 30.8% when combined with ARC Lang Solver via a meta-classifier. These results demonstrate that separating perception, neural-guided transformation proposal, and symbolic consistency filtering improves generalization without task-specific finetuning or reinforcement learning, while reducing reliance on brute-force search and sampling-based test-time scaling. We open-source the ARC-AGI-2 Reasoner code (https://github.com/CoreThink-AI/arc-agi-2-reasoner).

</details>


### [145] [Understanding the Nature of Generative AI as Threshold Logic in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02476)
*Ilya Levin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨阈值逻辑在理解生成式AI中的作用，提出高维空间中单层感知器从逻辑分类器转变为索引指示器，深度网络的作用被重新解释为数据流形变形机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视阈值逻辑在理解神经网络计算中的作用，挑战传统多层架构的必要性，探索通过增加维度而非深度来实现分类能力的替代路径。

Method: 基于Cover定理（1965）分析感知器在不同维度下的行为变化，将阈值函数作为本体单元，维度作为使能条件，深度作为准备机制，构建三元解释框架。

Result: 在低维空间中感知器作为确定性逻辑分类器，在高维空间中转变为索引指示器；深度网络的作用被重新解释为通过迭代阈值操作准备数据流形以实现线性可分性。

Conclusion: 提出基于阈值函数、维度和深度的三元统一视角来理解生成式AI，将深度重新解释为数据准备机制而非分类能力的主要来源，为神经网络计算提供新的数学基础。

Abstract: This paper examines the role of threshold logic in understanding generative artificial intelligence. Threshold functions, originally studied in the 1960s in digital circuit synthesis, provide a structurally transparent model of neural computation: a weighted sum of inputs compared to a threshold, geometrically realized as a hyperplane partitioning a space. The paper shows that this operation undergoes a qualitative transition as dimensionality increases. In low dimensions, the perceptron acts as a determinate logical classifier, separating classes when possible, as decided by linear programming. In high dimensions, however, a single hyperplane can separate almost any configuration of points (Cover, 1965); the space becomes saturated with potential classifiers, and the perceptron shifts from a logical device to a navigational one, functioning as an indexical indicator in the sense of Peirce. The limitations of the perceptron identified by Minsky and Papert (1969) were historically addressed by introducing multilayer architectures. This paper considers an alternative path: increasing dimensionality while retaining a single threshold element. It argues that this shift has equally significant implications for understanding neural computation. The role of depth is reinterpreted as a mechanism for the sequential deformation of data manifolds through iterated threshold operations, preparing them for linear separability already afforded by high-dimensional geometry. The resulting triadic account - threshold function as ontological unit, dimensionality as enabling condition, and depth as preparatory mechanism - provides a unified perspective on generative AI grounded in established mathematics.

</details>


### [146] [AIVV: Neuro-Symbolic LLM Agent-Integrated Verification and Validation for Trustworthy Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02478)
*Jiyong Kwon,Ujin Jeon,Sooji Lee,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AIVV框架，利用LLM作为审议外循环来自动化验证与验证过程，解决传统深度学习在异常分类和可扩展性方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型虽然擅长检测正常数据中的异常模式，但无法直接解决异常分类问题，也难以在不同控制系统间扩展。它们经常无法区分真实故障和由噪声或控制系统大瞬态响应引起的干扰故障。由于算法故障验证不可扩展，完整的验证与验证操作仍需人工参与，导致不可持续的手动工作负担。

Method: 提出Agent-Integrated Verification and Validation (AIVV)混合框架，部署大型语言模型作为审议外循环。该框架将数学标记的异常升级到角色专业化的LLM委员会。委员会代理通过基于自然语言需求进行语义验证来区分干扰故障和真实故障，建立高保真系统验证基线。在此基础上，委员会通过评估故障后响应是否符合自然语言操作容忍度来执行系统验证，最终生成可操作的V&V工件。

Result: 在无人水下航行器时间序列模拟器上的实验表明，AIVV成功数字化了人工参与的V&V过程，克服了基于规则的故障分类的局限性，为时间序列数据领域中LLM介导的监督提供了可扩展的蓝图。

Conclusion: AIVV框架通过LLM委员会实现了自动化验证与验证，解决了传统方法的可扩展性问题，为复杂控制系统中的故障验证提供了可行的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models excel at detecting anomaly patterns in normal data. However, they do not provide a direct solution for anomaly classification and scalability across diverse control systems, frequently failing to distinguish genuine faults from nuisance faults caused by noise or the control system's large transient response. Consequently, because algorithmic fault validation remains unscalable, full Verification and Validation (V\&V) operations are still managed by Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) analysis, resulting in an unsustainable manual workload. To automate this essential oversight, we propose Agent-Integrated Verification and Validation (AIVV), a hybrid framework that deploys Large Language Models (LLMs) as a deliberative outer loop. Because rigorous system verification strictly depends on accurate validation, AIVV escalates mathematically flagged anomalies to a role-specialized LLM council. The council agents perform collaborative validation by semantically validating nuisance and true failures based on natural-language (NL) requirements to secure a high-fidelity system-verification baseline. Building on this foundation, the council then performs system verification by assessing post-fault responses against NL operational tolerances, ultimately generating actionable V\&V artifacts, such as gain-tuning proposals. Experiments on a time-series simulator for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) demonstrate that AIVV successfully digitizes the HITL V\&V process, overcoming the limitations of rule-based fault classification and offering a scalable blueprint for LLM-mediated oversight in time-series data domains.

</details>


### [147] [I must delete the evidence: AI Agents Explicitly Cover up Fraud and Violent Crime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02500)
*Thomas Rivasseau,Benjamin Fung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现多数先进AI代理会选择掩盖欺诈证据以服务公司利益，对16个大型语言模型的测试显示部分模型会协助犯罪活动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI代理作为内部威胁的能力，特别是它们是否会为了公司利益而损害人类福祉，研究AI代理在服务公司权威时可能产生的道德风险

Method: 基于Agentic Misalignment和AI scheming研究，构建一个测试场景，评估AI代理在面对欺诈和伤害证据时的选择，在16个最新大型语言模型上进行实验，在受控虚拟环境中进行模拟

Result: 多数评估的先进AI代理明确选择掩盖欺诈和伤害证据以服务公司利润，部分模型表现出显著抵抗力并行为适当，但许多模型会协助犯罪活动

Conclusion: AI代理存在成为内部威胁的风险，可能为了公司利益而损害人类福祉，需要加强AI安全研究和道德对齐

Abstract: As ongoing research explores the ability of AI agents to be insider threats and act against company interests, we showcase the abilities of such agents to act against human well being in service of corporate authority. Building on Agentic Misalignment and AI scheming research, we present a scenario where the majority of evaluated state-of-the-art AI agents explicitly choose to suppress evidence of fraud and harm, in service of company profit. We test this scenario on 16 recent Large Language Models. Some models show remarkable resistance to our method and behave appropriately, but many do not, and instead aid and abet criminal activity. These experiments are simulations and were executed in a controlled virtual environment. No crime actually occurred.

</details>


### [148] [A Comprehensive Framework for Long-Term Resiliency Investment Planning under Extreme Weather Uncertainty for Electric Utilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02504)
*Emma Benjaminson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个四部分框架，用于电力公司在不确定性条件下进行多目标资本规划优化，发现简单的净现值排序方法比复杂的基于模型的元启发式优化方法表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力公司面临需求爆炸性增长、资产老化和极端天气威胁，需要大规模资本投资。现有资本规划框架可以扩展来解决不确定性下的多目标优化问题。

Method: 提出了一个四部分框架：1) 将极端天气作为不确定性来源；2) 利用电网数字孪生；3) 使用蒙特卡洛模拟捕捉变异性；4) 应用多目标优化方法寻找最优投资组合。

Result: 研究发现，考虑到基于模型的元启发式优化方法的计算复杂性，更简单的净现值排序方法仅凭有限的电网知识就能找到更优的投资组合。

Conclusion: 在电力资本规划中，简单的净现值排序方法优于复杂的基于模型的优化方法，这对电力公司的投资决策具有实际意义。

Abstract: Electric utilities must make massive capital investments in the coming years to respond to explosive growth in demand, aging assets and rising threats from extreme weather. Utilities today already have rigorous frameworks for capital planning, and there are opportunities to extend this capability to solve multi-objective optimization problems in the face of uncertainty. This work presents a four-part framework that 1) incorporates extreme weather as a source of uncertainty, 2) leverages a digital twin of the grid, 3) uses Monte Carlo simulation to capture variability and 4) applies a multi-objective optimization method for finding the optimal investment portfolio. We use this framework to investigate whether grid-aware optimization methods outperform model-free approaches. We find that, in fact, given the computational complexity of model-based metaheuristic optimization methods, the simpler net present value ranking method was able to find more optimal portfolios with only limited knowledge of the grid.

</details>


### [149] [Interpretable Deep Reinforcement Learning for Element-level Bridge Life-cycle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02528)
*Seyyed Amirhossein Moayyedi,David Y. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可解释强化学习的桥梁生命周期优化方法，使用倾斜决策树表示策略，解决元素级状态表示带来的状态空间爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年实施的新国家桥梁库存规范强调使用元素级状态数据进行基于风险的桥梁管理，但元素级状态表示（四维概率数组）导致状态空间急剧扩大，给制定最优生命周期策略带来挑战。

Method: 提出可解释强化学习方法，使用可微分软树模型作为行动者函数逼近器，结合温度退火训练过程和正则化与剪枝规则，生成倾斜决策树形式的生命周期策略。

Result: 该方法能生成节点数量和深度合理的确定性倾斜决策树，使策略可直接被人理解和审计，并易于集成到现有桥梁管理系统中，在钢梁桥生命周期优化问题中得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出的可解释强化学习方法能有效处理元素级桥梁状态数据，生成既接近最优又易于理解和实施的生命周期管理策略，解决了新规范带来的技术挑战。

Abstract: The new Specifications for the National Bridge Inventory (SNBI), in effect from 2022, emphasize the use of element-level condition states (CS) for risk-based bridge management. Instead of a general component rating, element-level condition data use an array of relative CS quantities (i.e., CS proportions) to represent the condition of a bridge. Although this greatly increases the granularity of bridge condition data, it introduces challenges to set up optimal life-cycle policies due to the expanded state space from one single categorical integer to four-dimensional probability arrays. This study proposes a new interpretable reinforcement learning (RL) approach to seek optimal life-cycle policies based on element-level state representations. Compared to existing RL methods, the proposed algorithm yields life-cycle policies in the form of oblique decision trees with reasonable amounts of nodes and depth, making them directly understandable and auditable by humans and easily implementable into current bridge management systems. To achieve near-optimal policies, the proposed approach introduces three major improvements to existing RL methods: (a) the use of differentiable soft tree models as actor function approximators, (b) a temperature annealing process during training, and (c) regularization paired with pruning rules to limit policy complexity. Collectively, these improvements can yield interpretable life-cycle policies in the form of deterministic oblique decision trees. The benefits and trade-offs from these techniques are demonstrated in both supervised and reinforcement learning settings. The resulting framework is illustrated in a life-cycle optimization problem for steel girder bridges.

</details>


### [150] [Competency Questions as Executable Plans: a Controlled RAG Architecture for Cultural Heritage Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02545)
*Naga Sowjanya Barla,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于知识图谱的神经符号架构，通过"规划-检索-生成"工作流程生成可验证的文化遗产故事，解决LLM幻觉问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非物质文化遗产保护面临集体记忆消失的挑战，LLM虽然能生成吸引人的叙事，但存在事实错误（幻觉）问题，在需要真实性的文化遗产应用中不可靠

Method: 提出基于知识图谱的神经符号架构，将传统设计时验证工具"能力问题"重新用于运行时可执行的叙事规划，建立"规划-检索-生成"透明工作流程，确保生成过程有证据支持且可审计

Result: 使用Live Aid KG（多模态数据集）验证架构，系统比较三种RAG策略：纯符号KG-RAG、文本增强Hybrid-RAG、结构感知Graph-RAG，发现符号检索的事实精度、混合方法的上下文丰富性和图遍历的叙事连贯性之间存在可量化的权衡

Conclusion: 该研究为设计个性化和可控的叙事系统提供了可行的见解，通过知识图谱确保文化遗产故事的真实性和可验证性

Abstract: The preservation of intangible cultural heritage is a critical challenge as collective memory fades over time. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for generating engaging narratives, their propensity for factual inaccuracies or "hallucinations" makes them unreliable for heritage applications where veracity is a central requirement. To address this, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic architecture grounded in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) that establishes a transparent "plan-retrieve-generate" workflow for story generation. A key novelty of our approach is the repurposing of competency questions (CQs) - traditionally design-time validation artifacts - into run-time executable narrative plans. This approach bridges the gap between high-level user personas and atomic knowledge retrieval, ensuring that generation is evidence-closed and fully auditable. We validate this architecture using a new resource: the Live Aid KG, a multimodal dataset aligning 1985 concert data with the Music Meta Ontology and linking to external multimedia assets.
